# E02-R2 residual ledger

Complete denominator: 225 B + 57 LA = 282 unique findings. Every row retains the Appendix C/E02 source status, the latest supported finding status, residual owner, evidence path@SHA, and any R class or reviewed P triage. `closed` rows are limited to their cited bounded receipt; the pending four-base R2 replay may reopen them only on an attributed discriminator. Rows whose current behavior is not measured remain explicitly partial, held or UNRUN; LA-021 is closed only within its measured corpus/vector profile.

## Discrepancies first

- Appendix C source counts remain 132 closed_bounded, 118 partial, 14 held, and 18 without a finding-level record. The final E02 r2–r84 audit preserves 71 of the 132 bounded items with item-named accepted receipts; 61 remain partial for the specific row-level record gaps stated in their rows. LA-021 closes within its measured bounded profile. The accepted no-record review classifies B09, B27, B72 and B111 as open; 13 other B items remain partial.
- The prior blanket downgrade of all 132 closed_bounded candidates is withdrawn. A row remains closed_bounded where a final receipt names that finding; lack of a receipt binding is recorded by ID and is not represented as a behavior failure.
- R1 N4 owner context is `not_established`: `_build_cycle_substrate_context_from_owner` uses a separate `.tmp/gy-s-composed-wmr-cas` store, so runtime tenant custody and caller provenance are unproven. N7 has no owner-issued post-growth context. Candidate-band work proceeds with a typed unknown; S8 authority remains blocked.
- B37 disagrees with Appendix C's package-level DUR-01 lifecycle sentence: the source card concerns `FileBudgetLedger` snapshot atomicity, empty/corrupt reads, and preserving budget limits/spend. Current source inspection shows an atomic replacement path, but named reader/interruption/corruption selectors and four-base replay are UNRUN; this is budget-accounting integrity, not policy-justification custody evidence.
- Crosswalk owner-scope census: 291 finding/bundle memberships × (77 standing GY task scopes + 22 Atlas slice scopes + 74 active A/B open/blocked register rows) = 50,343 membership-target audit opportunities. These are not distinct pairs; 282 findings × 173 targets = 48,786 distinct finding-target pairs. The register target set excludes 103 terminal A/B rows, one C blocked/unmerged row and one F ambiguous row. Thirty-three exact relations are admitted. The remaining 50,310 membership-target opportunities have no admitted edge in this scan; they are not residual findings, and this is not proof of no dependency. B201/B202→DS16 noun-only and unsupported LA-046→N13b/DS15 edges are removed. LA-046 touches GY-N7's scoped requirement-compilation operation. B02→N4, B01/B02→N6, B02/B03→N5, B02→N8, B06/B07→N5 and B09/B27/B28→N6 are task-operation touches only; LA-043/LA-044 touch Atlas DS3 through CLI-01 runtimeApiClient.ts/.js paths; B01→N4 was removed because its HTTP gate precedes N4 candidate generation. The live DS15 conflicts remain mapped to B12 only. Closed register row `acquisition-route-to-n13b-authority-binding` is excluded from the active-register denominator.
- The accepted P118 triage covers 118/118 partial rows: 76 engineering, 16 principal decisions, and 26 typed blockers. Triage is a next-step classification, not closure evidence.
- Current status is 72 closed, 192 partial, 14 held, and 4 open. This ledger pass ran no behavioral tests; except LA-021’s bounded probe and historical E02 accepted receipts, current four-base finding-level replays remain UNRUN.
- E02 r83/r84 `127/127 integrated_verified_bounded` is package-level acceptance; only the item-named evidence cited in each row supports the 71 preserved finding-level bounded closures.
- Phase 0 may require owner-authorized reissue of trust-posture pins, the canonical loaded-runtime manifest, and GY promotion-contract epochs. Nothing was restamped.
- R13 census: 2,693/2,693 `src/**/*.py` files parsed, zero parse errors; 26 direct `FileSystemCAS` calls plus 33 root-config `build_artifact_store` calls = 59 static constructions. E02’s five new direct roots are a subset; dynamic/path-forwarded constructions remain unresolved. The raw census is cited under this package’s ignored `raw/` directory.
- R14 same-source review identifies four stale-reference cases: one W5 historic repo ref and three WS-2D raw-client release-ledger refs. W5 remains `UNRUN/not_established` until the owner appends a sidecar binding immutable manifest SHA to the exact commit/tree; never restamp. WS-2D repair belongs to the release-ledger owner.
- The accepted held review keeps all 14 held rows in that state and refines named blockers; its lexical inventory is not treated as proof of semantic absence. Proposed distinguishing witnesses remain UNRUN.
- The accepted N/H and P review artifacts are cited by Git blob and reviewed branch commit while their files are not yet present in this worktree. This is branch provenance, not a claim that those review files are already tracked here.

## Status and evidence rules

- `closed` can retain an explicit bounded residual when the accepted test receipt names the item and its profile. It does not claim universal closure.
- `partial` records either an explicit incomplete property or a specific absence of item-bound accepted evidence; each row says which. `UNRUN` is not a pass and does not mean environmental red.
- Source-card, package-level acceptance and triage records alone do not close a finding. The 71 E02 closed-bounded rows cite the exact E02 final record and source bundle; LA-021 cites the separate reviewed probe.
- B73 remains partial because the native checkpoint path exists while a reachable legacy-hook path may publish state and completion markers at different boundaries. Its atomic crash/reopen witness and removal probe remain UNRUN.
